## §14 THE WAVES (each: car-0 · leaves · hosts · FIRST-VISIBLE (requirement 16) · TIER (17) · DEGRADED ARM · EDITOR (L10: (a) forks · (b) seals and conditions · (c) the counterparty · (d) the tick and the states · (e) the record and the vocabularies) · Alignment (13) · `causedBy` (20))

BD is a coupling CARRIER across **WAR · POPULATIONS · TRADE · INFO · FAITH** (and, from round 4, the INTERIOR — the commons, the guild, the sword-holder); its prefix `BD` is chartered with its first coupling-registry row (requirement 21(a)/(c)). HBF rows minted, each in its wave's commit: `bodyBirthDraw`, `bodyChoicePick`, `bodiesMeetOutcome`, `bodyRaidOutcome`, `bodySpillDraw`, and from round 4 `guildWarOutcome`, `bodyFissionDraw`, `sponsorDeliveryDraw` (the band contest's draws are the owning kinds').

**BD-0 — THE RECORD, THE MEASURE AND THE DM'S HAND.**
- **Car-0 (a MEASUREMENT before any BD line):** (1) re-measure `calamityKernel.js :: advanceCalamity` with MN's, ST's and PR's leaves and BD's call (461 + 4 × 3 ≤ 800) — ⛔ STOP at build if the host cannot carry a fourth leaf or if MN-0/ST-0/PR-0 chose another host (BD's stage then follows theirs); (2) measure the population: bodies a seed family would hold under the DRAFT caps (cost: DRAFT ≤ 2 ms per tick at fifteen towns, UNMEASURED; a per-snapshot memo on `bodyHabitat`) — and THE PER-TICK COST AT CONTINENT SCALE with the REAL reads (L-BD 49 (c)): round 4's PROXY (`cure/D-BD-r4-probe/tickcost.mjs`, executed: a radius BFS per body, a believed-coverage lookup per stretch in the radius, a seven-way pick, one encounter test per patrol in its country, the world cap one body per two towns) runs 0.16–0.27 ms per tick at 30 towns, 1.4–2.5 ms at 300, 20–38 ms at 3,000 and 78–150 ms at 10,000 (radius two to three hops; linear in bodies × radius area) — ⛔ STOP at build if the real reads at the largest supported world exceed the AGGREGATE per-tick budget (CHAIR-NOTES 18 (f)'s instrument); the CAP per region is the first lever, the RADIUS the second ; (3) measure ST's untied holders on the family's seeds (how many id-less authored gangs the adoption converts) . **Leaves:** `src/domain/bodies/bodyLedger.js` (the one writer; normalizer; ids; vocabularies; `adoptUntiedHolders`), `bodyReads.js` (§2.5), `bodyStage.js` (the stage, filled by BD-1..10), `bodyVerbs.js`. **Host edits:** `calamityKernel.js` 461 (+3: the call and the merge); `realmManifest` (eight rows) and `realmVerbExecution.js` 663 (the apply arms, the `subject` dial with the derived anchor); `display/armyStrength.js` 94 (`STRENGTH_BAND_KEYS` if MN-0 has not landed); `spatialUsage.js` 188 (the `bodies` and `hiddenWays` TRACKED rows). **FIRST-VISIBLE:** the adoption line the advance BD-0 lights on a world with untied bands — "The band at the Tolle ruin is the Tolle hill gang." — and a placed body's dossier line at its approval. **TIER:** canonized (gangs, beasts), aspatial-safe (a placed conclave); premium; the OMNISCIENT PRESET the same. **DEGRADED ARM:** dark. **EDITOR:** (a) forks — none in BD-0 (ids FNV, no draw); (b) seals and conditions — the predicate `bodyPresent(settlementId)` and the eight verbs' predicates (`realmVetoProse` reasons `body_gate_dark` · `body_unknown` · `body_unnamed` — a leader or a name only for a NAMED body, BD23 · `beast_never_named` — a beast takes no name and no leader; the compile's `beast_never_placed` RETIRED by L-BD 36); (c) the counterparty — verbs target real settlements only; every neighbour reader BD adds accepts a phantom partner without throwing; (d) the tick and the states — no institution or person writes; (e) the record and the vocabularies — the ledger is worldState-side lived history outside `rederive`; `BODY_KINDS · BODY_BIRTHS · BODY_ENDS · COURT_STANCES` are save-data contract (a rename withdraws pending decrees through §20.3's resolver). **Alignment:** alignment-empty WITH REASON — a body is not a court and has no constituents (its stances read alignments; they do not move them). **`causedBy`:** the adoption receipt's parent is ST's holder event; a placed body's is the verb's outcome id.

**BD-1 — BIRTHS, SUPPLIES AND THE WEEKLY CHOICE (gangs and beasts).**
- **Car-0:** birth (d) is DARK until WC-11/12's `brigand` event lands (RULINGS-ROUND3 §G.4 — the compile's B1-S1 measurement is RETIRED: the loser's deployment record and the lifted siege's receipt do not carry the defeat facts, so reading them would mint a second defeat read) ; measure `MIGRATION_REFUSALS`' outcomes reachable per tick (birth (e)). **Leaves:** `bodyHabitat.js`, `bodyBirths.js`, `bodyChoice.js`, `bodySupply.js`. **Hosts:** none beyond BD-0's stage. **FIRST-VISIBLE:** within a few weeks' advance on a family seed with a frontier town or a hungry quarter — "Out of Kell's quarter, a gang has taken to the hill roads." / "Wolves have come down into the Tolle hills." **TIER:** canonized; premium; the OMNISCIENT PRESET the same (no belief inside the birth). **DEGRADED ARM:** the record without births (BD-0's adoption and the DM's placements only). **EDITOR:** (a) forks `bodies:birth:<word>:<placeKey>:<tick>`, `bodies:<bodyId>:<tick>` — registered, HBF rows `bodyBirthDraw` · `bodyChoicePick`; (b) seals — a DM `BODY_HOLD` pins the choice; the conditions predicate `bodyBirthPossible`; (c) the counterparty — births name their source settlement (real) or site; (d) the tick — births and ends in the stage's fixed order; (e) `BODY_CHOICES` closed per kind. **Alignment:** a conclave's choice reads its god's axes (`stanceOf`, `deityTemper`); a gang's reads the town's tolerance; neither writes alignment. **`causedBy`:** every birth names its source event (the spill's crime-pressure row, the battle receipt, the refusal, the season); every end names its fact.

**BD-2 — THE FEED (the re-feed; `bodyEffectsEnabled`).**
- **Car-0:** confirm the presence composer has landed (A.3; else BD-2 BUILDS it on the first-to-land idiom at `spatial/presenceComposer.js` with MN §3.3's sentence verbatim) and the walker over its closed registration list; measure that `espionageMissions.js`'s `covertCompetence01` call holds `worldState` (else the `ctx` arg rides from its caller one hop up); measure that the two arrival sites take the optional fired-loss sink without a signature change; measure the RE-FEED on a seed family (latch counts, level distribution, loss totals, smuggling outcomes, before and after) for the owner's signature — and the DECLARED LOOP of §22 A2: whether a pulse-born `monster_raider_pressure` reaches `activeConditions` at runtime (its `war_pressure` archetype's +0.16 on the defence pressure; `cure/D-BD-r5.md` m6, PLAUSIBLE) and the loop's gain into births through `bodySecurity` (pin: ≤ the watch weight × 0.16, bounded by the cap and the dwell) . **Leaves:** `bodyPresence.js` (`bodyPresenceFeeder`, registered), `criminalPower.js`, `bodyGateRows.js` (the raider-gate row), `bodyAttribution.js`. **Host edits:** `presenceComposer.js` (one registration row); `supplyKernel.js` 489 (`guildOf` → `criminalPowerOf`, one line); `espionageGauntlet.js` 309 (one line); `espionageMath.js` 163 (the defaulted `ctx`); `stressorGates.js` 609 (one row in `monsterRaiderGate`); `commodityFlow.js` 474 · `supplyShipments.js` 210 (the optional sink push, one line each). **FIRST-VISIBLE:** the same advance a `capable` gang raids an unwatched country — "The hill road has gone to the Tolle gang; Kell's grain came in short." **TIER:** canonized (the embattlement gate); the criminal-power composition aspatial-safe (it reads the record only); premium. **DEGRADED ARM:** today's ramp, today's criminal power, today's raider births (byte-identical). **EDITOR:** (a) forks — none new (the landed rolls fire as before; attribution is a pure read); (b) seals — a DM `let_pass` on `traffic_waylaid` lifts the row (§9.6); (c) the counterparty — the attribution names a real body or none; (d) the tick — read-last/write-next (the composer reads the ledger as last week's stage left it); (e) the composer's registration list (a walker). **Alignment:** none (danger and crime are not alignment). **`causedBy`:** a fired loss attributed to a body carries the body's act as its parent.

**BD-3 — THE CONCLAVES (the gate, not the curb).**
- **Car-0:** measure `religiousContest.js`'s strength line for one bounded multiplier (493 + 1 ≤ 800); measure that the urban band's roster carries a religious institution that follows a creed's standing (BD17's "power in institutions" — else ROUTE WF the link); measure WF-5b's landing state (`covert`) — if unbuilt, `hide` in town is the strength lean alone (recorded). **Leaves:** `conclaveLean.js`, `courtStance.js` (the fork and `body_stance`), `conclaveMission.js`. **Host edits:** `religiousContest.js` 493 (one multiplier at `strengthByRef[dref]`); `stressorGates.js` (one row in `religiousConversionGate`). **FIRST-VISIBLE:** within a season on a deity-bearing seed with two creeds of near gods — "The Grey Lady's faithful came to Ashford from Kell and were welcomed." **TIER:** aspatial-safe (the in-town lean, the LOCAL lane); missions `faithSpreadEnabled` (dramatic and full) and canon for the road; deity-free worlds dark (FPQ-62: the birth preset seats no deity). **DEGRADED ARM:** the faith system's own dynamics (byte-identical). **EDITOR:** (a) the court's fork is draw-free (`body_stance`'s tilt is a bias on `net`); (b) seals — the DM's `BODY_STANCE`; (c) the counterparty — a real court and a creed key; (d) the tick — the stance moves only at a hearing's resolution; (e) `COURT_STANCES` closed. **Alignment:** BOTH DIRECTIONS READ, NONE WRITTEN: the stance reads the two gods' axes; the faith's lean on the town's alignment stays the faith system's own (requirement 13's coupling is the faith system's). **`causedBy`:** a `dissolved` flock's entry names the body; a court's stance names its hearing.

**BD-4 — ENGAGEMENTS (`bodies_meet` · `raid` · sites · patrols · `traffic_waylaid` · caravans · supply).**
- **Car-0:** confirm each widened kind's owner wave has landed or carries BD's producer row (ST-2, PR-2, WY-6, the war volume's `raid`/`supply_raid`); where an owner has not landed, BD's producer waits (no kind built twice; the first-to-land owns); PIN THE DEFENCE MATRIX (S10) on the family's generated settlements before the producer lights — `bodyDefenceOf` per tier × might band at the DRAFT margin, the walls-ruined arm included (`cure/D-BD-r5-probe/defence-matrix.mjs` is the executed precedent) . **Leaves:** `bodiesMeet.js`, `bodyRaid.js`, `bodyTraffic.js`, `defenceRead.js` (`bodyDefenceOf`, the one body-scale defence read: `defenseLedger`'s `military`/`monster`, the live collapse, ST's keep term — B.3). **Host edits:** none (registrations in the owners' commits). **FIRST-VISIBLE:** the week a raid lands — "The Tolle gang has come down on Low Ford for its grain." **TIER:** canonized; `patrol_engages` with `patrolsEnabled`; sites with `siteFatesEnabled`; caravans with WY-6's flags; `supply_raid` and armies with `warLayerEnabled`. **DEGRADED ARM:** acts resolve as receipts (no hearings) where `hearingWeekEnabled` is dark. **EDITOR:** (a) forks `bodies_meet:<pairKey>:<tick>`, `raid:<bodyId>:<targetId>:<tick>` (HBF `bodiesMeetOutcome`, `bodyRaidOutcome`); (b) the hearing seals; (c) real anchors; (d) one meeting per pair per week; (e) the three kinds' verdict lines. **Alignment:** a flock's fight with a gang reads its god (§4.2); none written. **`causedBy`:** each verdict's receipt names the act.

**BD-5 — THE HIDDEN WAYS AND THE HUNT (`hiddenWaysEnabled`).**
- **Car-0:** measure the CANDIDATE geometry on a seed family (corridors beside roads and BYPASS corridors per country, none on plain; capacity) against the map's `wild` layer where it exists; confirm master's ladder as measured (`ROUTE_GRADES`, `HIDDEN_PATH_KINDS`, `gradeLegMultiplier('hidden')` 2 pinned to `HIDDEN_PATH_SLOWDOWN` 2) and land the ONE declared token `body` in `HIDDEN_PATH_KINDS` with the franchise's own walker (⛔ STOP at build if that list's walker forbids a non-person kind — then the access law is the chair's); confirm PR's `pursue` widening, its gleaning roll and the junction read landed (PR's text), IN's brokerage sale of a way (IN's commit), the route lifecycle's use counter input (WY's commit) . **Leaves:** `hiddenWays.js` (the candidates, the one ladder read `hiddenWayEdges`, knowledge and its freshness, its writer), `bodyRoute.js` (`bodyRouteChoice`, BD11), `bodyHabits.js` (`BODY_HABITS`). **Host edits:** `routeNetworkConsumers.js` 184 (ONE token, `body`, a DECLARED change on a landed access law); none else (PR's coverage reads the knowledge through PR's own commit). **FIRST-VISIBLE:** the week a patrol catches a body on a trail — "Kell's riders caught the Tolle gang on the drovers' path; the watch knows the way now." **TIER:** canonized; the map's canon overrides when it supplies a layer (the ask). **DEGRADED ARM:** every body on the road. **EDITOR:** (a) none new; (b) the DM's `TRAIL_KNOWN`; (c) ways between real nodes; (d) knowledge fades weekly on HB's curve (refreshed by use, watching, a catch, a purchase); (e) `TRAIL_LEARNED` (six), the ladder's rung words (the map's). **Alignment:** none. **`causedBy`:** a learned way names the catch, the survey, the purchase, the pursuit or the DM; a choice of network names its top weight.

**BD-6 — SPILLOVER AND RESPONSIBILITY (`bodySpillEnabled`).**
- **Car-0:** measure PL's `requestFealtyRow` and the `body:` prefix (PL's commit); measure GR's demand producer row (GR's); measure the sword lean's car into `applyFactionPatch` (775 + ≤ 1); export `generosityKernel.js :: faithProximity` (an `export` keyword, +0 effective lines at the 800 ceiling) or pin BD's leaf's formula to it (B.19) . **Leaves:** `bodySpill.js` (incl. the `shared_faith` lean), `publicOrder.js`. **Host edits:** `factionCompetition.js` (≤ 1 line, the leaf-first car); `generosityKernel.js` (+0, a keyword); the patches ride `applyRelationshipPatch` (no host line). **FIRST-VISIBLE:** the season a neighbour stays embattled — "Ashford blames Kell for the brigands in its hills." **TIER:** aspatial-safe for the patches (they read the record and the graph); the habitat spill canonized; the liege face `polityObligationsEnabled`. **DEGRADED ARM:** no spill beyond the bodies' own moves. **EDITOR:** (a) fork `bodies:spill:<N>:<T>:<tick>` (HBF `bodySpillDraw`); (b) a DM pin on the country holds it; (c) real edges only; (d) once per pair per body per dwell; (e) the incident types `peace_unkept` (with `bodyKind`) · `peace_kept` [r4: `brigandage_exported` retired, L-BD 23b]. **Alignment:** the sword-holder's lean is a faction power move, not alignment; none written. **`causedBy`:** every patch names the body and the spill.

**BD-7 — WARTIME (the irregulars, the supply road, the deserters, the armies and the menace's recruits).**

|---|---|---|---|
| create | the stage's births and BD-0's adoption; the DM's `PLACE_BODY`; one writer `bodyLedger.js` | a catch, a survey product, a guide, a BROKER's sale, a PURSUIT's gleaned prefix, the DM [r5]; `watched` by THE PATROLS' stage; one writer `hiddenWays.js` | the word step of the stage (and the rumor road's carriage) |
| read | §2.5's reads; the composer's row; PR's encounter and coverage; ST's holder reads | `knownTrailsOf`'s readers — PR's coverage and prediction, PS's `hiddenWayFor`, CH's and ES's routing, a wartime unit's risk read, the body's route choice, the map's way layer [r5, B.13] | `believedBodies` |
| persist | the conditional `spatialLedgers` idiom (drop-when-empty); normalized on load (unknown kinds dropped with a receipt) | the same | the landed rumor record |
| regenerate | lived history: a regeneration keeps the ledger (worldState-side, outside `rederive`); a body whose source settlement no longer exists keeps its record (its `birth.fromId` names history) | kept; a trail whose nodes left the digest is dropped at normalization | kept (the rumor TTL) |
| undo | the proposal ring's snapshot restore (the landed undo); verb inverses are DM acts | the same | the same |
| import | the campaign import carries `spatialLedgers` whole; a body naming a missing settlement is normalized to roaming at its nearest real node | carried; unknown towns dropped | carried |
| migrate | none at landing (a new key); when the map's hidden-ways layer lands, trail keys re-point to the canon's ids (the slot recorded, BD20) | re-keyed at the canon's adoption (the slot) | none |
| dark-after-lit | the ledger STAYS (lived history) and NOTHING READS IT: the composer's row reads zero, `criminalPowerOf` returns the landed value, the gate rows absent, ST's holder refs read as untied holders with the record's `might` (ST's own dark arm) — except a CONCLAVE's site, which reads as a `faith` holder of its CREED (a church's site), never as untied ; relighting resumes from the stored records | stays, unread (PR's coverage reads as dark) | stays; the dossier's voice unread |
| death | a body's end writes its `ended` row (ring 24) and drops its record, its pairs and its stance rows; its site falls EMPTY through ST's writer; a SETTLEMENT's death drops that court's `courtStances` and re-homes bodies whose `homeId` it was (to none) | a dead town's knowledge is dropped | untouched |
| veil | a body is in no court's belief until witnessed (BD12); a body at a `keptSecret` site is court-veiled by ST's veil; the DM's view always shows the truth | per-town belief by construction | per-observer by construction |
| horizon (req. 23) | `records` ≤ the world cap; `pairs` ≤ (records choose 2), dropped with a body; `courtStances` ≤ towns × (bodies + creeds); `births` keys dropped one dwell after their tick; `ended` 24 | knowledge FADES on HB's curve and LAPSES within one tenth of neutral unless used, watched or refreshed (L-BD 11f; the round-4a fixed 52 weeks struck); the office's ways never fade while the office is held [r5] | the rumor TTL |

## §18 WHAT A DM READS (the Evidence Law: who, which power, what effect; deeds only, never forecasts; no numerals; no mechanism names)

1. "Out of Kell's quarter, a gang has taken to the hill roads." — surface (one Herald line); scrutiny: its birth card (hungry streets, a thin watch, a strong guild).
2. "Formed from the deserters of Kell's levy, a gang holds the Tolle hills." — surface; scrutiny: the battle it came from.
3. "The hill road has gone to the Tolle gang; Kell's grain came in short." — surface; scrutiny: the country's card (the gang's size and hunger, its base at the ruin, the riders on the low road).
4. "The Tolle gang has come down on Low Ford for its grain." — the raid card (CLIMAX).
5. "The Grey Lady's faithful came to Ashford from Kell and were welcomed; they keep a shrine by the mill." — surface; scrutiny: the two gods' leanings, the court's welcome.
6. "Merewick has outlawed the Grey Lady's flock; her faithful there pray behind shutters, and they are angry." — surface; scrutiny: the followers' unrest, the court's standing among them.
7. "The Grey Lady's faithful in Kell refused the hill gang's demand and sent word to the watch." — scrutiny line on the flock's card.
8. "Kell's riders caught the Tolle gang on the drovers' path; the watch knows the way now." — surface; the trail drawn behind the toggle.
9. "The wolves of the Tolle hills came down to the road in the hard frost." — surface (winter); in spring: "The wolves of the Tolle hills have whelped."
10. "The hill roads are quiet; the quarter still breeds." — scrutiny only (the balance line).
11. "Ashford blames Kell for the brigands in its hills." — surface (a relationship line); scrutiny: the gang's base in Kell's land, the season it has lasted.
12. "With Kell's riders gone to the war, the Tolle gang rides the low road openly." — surface (wartime).
13. "Out of the Tolle hills, the brigands have a name now: the Hollow Road gang." — surface (the naming receipt, once) [r4].
14. "Kell has put a price on the Hollow Road gang." — surface (`body_bounty` `posted`) [r4].
15. "Kell's warden has taken a troop of riders after the Hollow Road gang; he knows the drover's way." — surface (`hunt_taken` `undertaken`; the troop's size is on the card, never a numeral in the line) [r4].

- **WY / the route lifecycle:** (a) the lifecycle takes BD's USE COUNTER (one per body crossing of a candidate or a way) as an input to the rung's rise (wild → way → trail) and the desire-path birth (L-BD 11h); (b) the map contract's six rung words and the junction rule (a chartered road may start or end at a junction); (c) BD's bypass candidates as the host's fallback geometry until the map's `wild` layer lands.
- **EC:** the gauntlet's run CHOOSES a way by BD11's roll (the gate's stance, the believed coverage, the way's time and capacity), caught only on a known and watched way, a passed run costing the holder its toll (L-BD 11b; the landed `smuggler` franchise).
- **ES:** the operative's ROUTING ARM on the sending court's `knownTrailsOf` (belief), the routing as the mission's cover and cost; a caught operative's confession gives up its ways (`by_catch`) (L-BD 11c).
- **IN:** the brokerage SELLS specific ways from its belief stock with a freshness (IN's telling age), priced by IN's model, neutral (it sells a gang where the watch moved) — BD writes `by_broker` (L-BD 11g).
- **PT:** nothing new (RU-13/RU-14 applied in BD: `END_BODY`'s `party` dial, `TRAIL_KNOWN`'s fourth dial, the body half's words).
- **FOLD (P4):** §5 registers master's `hidden` grade and `HIDDEN_PATH_KINDS` (+`body`), the map's six rungs and the class, `junction:<id>` as a key under `road (a, b)` beside `stretch:` and `trail:` (B.4 · SEAMS-r4-16), `confront`, `by_broker · by_pursuit`, `shared_faith`, `guild_war` (the condition's archetype, aligned with the kind), `ROAD_COVER_BY_MIGHT`; §3.4's `raid` row ref gains `bodyKind` (BD-r4-22) and the hostile flock as a raider (B.2); the `ransom` row's closings as B.7's partition; §3.1.16 WC-12's ramp term = BD's row (BD-r4-12); §6 C17's BD-5 row names the route lifecycle and IN's brokerage as prerequisites of the rungs and the market; §7 ⭐ OD-BD-49 (the declared token) visible.
- **The map (HOST-asks):** confirm a junction is a point on ONE primary hop (its `a|b`), so the card's WHERE stays `road (a, b)` (SEAMS-r4-16); the bypass corridors' geometry is the map's to draw.

PR §2.6 (`trailWatchOf`), §8.1 (the body traveller), the placement's `lived` rows — BD §2.3 writes `watched` through PR's stage and refreshes on `lived`; PR's `hunt` (three sites) and its `cover01` from the round-4a graph → ROUTED. PS l.107 · 329–333 · 582 · 607 still price BD's struck 1.5 → ROUTED (B.4). ST §6.1 step 1 (an ended body's site `cleared`; a body's own move `abandoned`) and its 8-row `events` ring — BD §6.4 and §6.8 (c) now use ST's words; A.2's re-point is ST's cure. PL §4.8 (1) (`peace_unkept` in the REQUESTED family, the episode `body:<kind>:<hash>:<tick>` = BD's `bodyId + ':' + tick`) — BD §10.2 (b) agrees. WC §1.8.1 (the read-point; the ramp term) — BD14 and §22 A2 agree; WC's own text owes the one-term bracket → ROUTED. Every remaining difference is a routed half the ruling itself assigns to the sibling; BD opened none of its own.

## §22 THE STRESSOR COVERAGE TABLE — every act, every habitat input, every counterforce, tied to the crisis truth the world already knows (BD28; the owner, 18:43: "make sure that everything that we jsut designed is also appropraetly tied to respective exisitng stressors, be comprehensive in that and cohesive."; MN4's law; RULINGS-ROUND4 B: this volume's LAST section and the seams skeptic's first target) 

Measured substrate (master `81f1cd963`): twenty-one stressor types (`stressorsCore.js`), twenty spawn gates (`STRESSOR_SPAWN_GATES`, `[0.1, 3]` by `gateResult`'s clamp), the counterforce table (`STRESSOR_COUNTERFORCES`), the promotion to conditions (`conditionPromotion.js :: promoteStressorsToConditions`); NO embattled stressor exists on master (the gauge is the embattlement ledger; WC-12's designed brigand ramp term is WC's) and no criminal-power stressor (the criminal power is a read, `criminalStrength01Of`). BD mints NO stressor kind, NO condition and NO crisis law; every row below rides a landed record or is a named SLOT.
